                                                                                                   DEFINITIONS

「A股」                               指   本公司每股面值人民幣1.00元的普通股，以人民幣認購並於深交所上市（股
                                       份代號：002460）
 “A Share(s)”                          ordinary share(s) of the Company, with a nominal value of RMB1.00 each, which
                                       are subscribed for in RMB and listed on the SZSE (stock code: 002460)

「公司章程」                             指   本公司的組織章程
 “Articles of Association”             the articles of association of the Company

「審核委員會」                            指   本公司的審核委員會
 “Audit Committee”                     the audit committee of the Company

「董事會」                              指   董事會
 “Board”                               the board of Directors

「《企業管治守則》        」                 指   香港上市規則附錄C1所載之《企業管治守則》
 “Corporate Governance Code”           the Corporate Governance Code set out in Appendix C1 of the Hong Kong
                                       Listing Rules

「本公司」、「公司」或「我們」                    指   江西贛鋒鋰業集團股份有限公司，一間於中國成立的股份有限公司，其A股（股
                                       份代號：002460）及H股（股份代號：1772）分別於深交所及聯交所主板上市
 “Company” “our Company” or “we”       Ganfeng Lithium Group Co., Ltd., a joint stock company with limited liability
                                       established in the PRC whose A Shares (stock code: 002460) and H Shares
                                       (stock code: 1772) are listed on the SZSE and on the Main Board of the Stock
                                       Exchange, respectively

「董事」                               指   本公司董事
 “Director(s)”                         the director(s) of the Company

「贛鋒國際」                             指   GFL International Co., Limited, 一間於2011年3月29日在香港註冊成立的私人
                                       股份有限公司，為本公司的全資附屬公司
 “GFL International”                   GFL International Co., Limited, a private company limited by shares incorporated
                                       in Hong Kong on 29 March 2011 and a wholly-owned subsidiary of our
                                       Company

「本集團」                              指   本公司及其附屬公司
 “Group”                               the Company and its subsidiaries

「H股」                               指   本公司股本中每股面值人民幣1.00元的境外上市外資股，於聯交所主板上市
                                       並以港元買賣（股份代號：1772）
 “H Share(s)”                          overseas listed foreign shares in the share capital of the Company, with a
                                       nominal value of RMB1.00 each, which are listed on the Main Board of the Stock
                                       Exchange and traded in Hong Kong dollars (stock code: 1772)

                                       Kong Limited

 “Hong Kong”                           the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region of the PRC

「LCE」                              指   碳酸鋰當量

     MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS

     行業回顧                                                             INDUSTRY REVIEW

     1.    鋰資源市場分析                                                    1.   Analysis of lithium resource market

           全球鋰資源供給大部分來自鹽湖和硬                                                The majority of global lithium resources are sourced from salt lakes
           岩 鋰 礦。成 熟 的 鹽 湖 主 要 分 佈 在 南 美                                   and hard rock lithium mines. Well-developed salt lakes are mainly
           鋰 三 角 和 中 國，大 部 分 鋰 礦 山 依 然 集                                   found in the lithium delta of South America and in China, while the
           中 在 澳 大 利 亞。近 年 來，在 終 端 市 場                                     majority of lithium mines are concentrated in Australia. In recent
           的 需 求 刺 激 下，鋰 資 源 的 投 資 開 發 力                                   years, stimulated by demand in the end-user market, there has been
           度 加 大，供 應 逐 漸 多 元 化。根 據 長 江                                     an increased investment and development in lithium resources,
           證 券 研 究 所 的 數 據，2025年 預 計 全 球                                   leading to a diversification of supply. According to the data from
           鋰資源供應量為150.2萬噸碳酸鋰當量                                             Yangtze Securities Research Institute, the global supply of lithium
          （「 LCE」），同 比 增 長14.6%，其 中 鋰                                      resource is expected to reach 1,502,000 tons of lithium carbonate
           輝 石、鹽 湖 及 鋰 雲 母 來 源 分 別 是76.0                                   equivalent (“LCE”) in 2025, representing a year-on-year increase of
           萬 噸LCE，56.7萬 噸LCE及17.5萬 噸                                       14.6%, among which 760,000 tons of LCE, 567,000 tons of LCE and
           LCE，分別佔比51%，38%及11%。                                            175,000 tons of LCE are sourced from spodumene, salt lakes and
                                                                           lepidolite, accounting for 51%, 38% and 11%, respectively.

行業回顧（續）                               INDUSTRY REVIEW (Continued)

1.   鋰資源市場分析（續）                       1.   Analysis of lithium resource market (Continued)

     (1)   鋰輝石精礦市場                         (1)   Market of spodumene concentrate

           澳大利亞是全球最大的鋰礦石生                        Australia is one of the world’s largest producers of lithium ore,
           產國之一，當地礦業發達、法律                        with a well-developed mining industry, comprehensive laws
           法規齊全、基礎設施良好。根據                        and regulations, and good infrastructure. According to the data
           Fastmarkets的 數 據，截 至2025              from Fastmarkets, as of June 2025, the domestic CIF price of
           年6月，5%-6%鋰 輝 石 精 礦 的 中                5%-6% spodumene concentrate was around USD620-640
           國 到 岸 價 格 約 合620-640美 元╱              per ton, representing a decrease of 25%-31% from the price
           噸，較2025年 初 價 格850-900美                of USD850-900 per ton at the beginning of 2025. In Australia,
           元╱噸下跌25%-31%。澳大利亞                     being an important source of global lithium resources, the
           作為全球鋰礦資源的重要產地，                        supply of lithium ore from major producers is relatively stable.
           主要生產商的鋰礦供應量相對穩                        In the first half of 2025, Australian mines actively promoted
           定。2025年上半年，澳大利亞礦                      cost optimization efforts to adapt to the current market
           山積極推動成本優化工作，以適                        environment. Although overall production remained stable,
           應當前市場環境，雖然整體生產                        there was still some pressure on production and operations
           維持穩定，但在當前市場行情下                        under the current market conditions, and it is expected that
           仍有一定的生產經營壓力，預計                        the supply of lithium pyroxene in Australia for the whole year
           全年澳大利亞鋰輝石的供給量依                        is still uncertain. Lithium resources in the African continent are
           然存在不確定性。非洲大陸鋰資                        primarily distributed across countries such as Congo (Kinshasa),
           源主要分佈在剛果（金）、馬里、                       Mali and Zimbabwe, a significant portion of existing production
           津巴布韋等多個國家，現有產能                        capacity is concentrated in the hands of small and medium-
           有很大一部分集中在中小礦商                         sized miners. It is different from the lithium market in Australia,
           手中，這與澳洲鋰礦市場主要由                        which is dominated by large miners. Although the absolute
           大型礦商主導的情況有所不同。                        amount of lithium resources is not as large as that of Australia,
           盡管在鋰資源的絕對數量上不                         there are world-class lithium mining projects in Africa, which has
           如澳大利亞，非洲地區也擁有世                        abundant resources of spodumene and petalite, with high ore
           界級的鋰礦項目，這些項目具備                        grades. Nevertheless, the development of the African lithium
           豐富的鋰輝石和透鋰長石資源，                        market still faces some challenges. As lithium mining projects
           礦石品位較高。但非洲鋰礦市場                        in Africa are scattered in many different countries, and due to
           的開發依然面臨一些挑戰，由於                        the influence of geopolitical uncertainties, complex geological
           非洲鋰礦項目分散在多個不同國                        structures and lagging development of supporting infrastructure,
           家，受地緣政治多變、地質結構                        the level of exploration of lithium resources is relatively low

     European Commission   Carbon Emissions            In April 2025, the European Commission pro-posed
                             Assessment System            amendments to the carbon emissions assessment
                             Amendment                    system. The new regulations will change the annual
                                                          carbon emissions assessment for automakers to an
                                                          average assessment of carbon emissions over a three-year
                                                          assessment period (2025–2027), and will resume annual
                                                          carbon emissions assessments after 2027. On 8 May
                                                          2025, the European Parliament and member negotiated
                                                          and reached an agreement on the proposed changes.
                                                          The amendments will officially take effect by the end of
                                                          2025. The carbon emissions legislation imposes significant
                                                          compliance pressures on the supply side in the long term.
                           推遲《歐盟電池與廢電池                 2025年5月，歐盟委員會今日正式提交提案，擬將《歐盟電

                                                          新生效日期定為2027年8月18日。歐盟本計劃在8月實施
                                                          的電池法案中的電池供應鏈盡職調查環節被延期兩年，

           German Government               Electric Company Vehicle     In April 2025, Germany’s new government will implement
                                             Tax Reform                    tax reforms to expand the tax ad-vantages of electric
                                                                           company vehicles, including (1) increasing tax preference
                                                                           for company vehicles, raising the upper limit of vehicle
                                                                           prices from €70,000 to €100,000; (2) establishing a special
                                                                           depreciation policy for electric vehicles to provide additional
                                                                           tax preference for purchasers; and (3) extending the tax
                                                                           exemption for electric vehicles to 2035.
           意大利政府                           電動車「以舊換新」新政                  2025年6月意大利政府宣佈即將啟動一項新的綠色激勵計
                                                                           劃，鼓勵用電動車替代燃油車。該計劃獲歐盟批准，並
                                                                           納入國家復甦與韌性計劃(PNRR)第五次修訂中。此次新
                                                                           政策的目標是替換約3.9萬輛燃油車為電動車。政府有6
                                                                           億歐元的預算原用於安裝充電樁。該計劃將必須在2026
                                                                           年6月30日之前用完資金。
                                                                        補貼標準：ISEE         （家庭年收入）
                                                                        補貼金額：ISEE低於或等於3萬歐元的納稅人，可獲得電動
                                                                           汽車1.1萬歐元的補貼；ISEE在3萬至4萬歐元之間的納稅
                                                                           人，補貼金額降至9,000歐元。
           Italian Government              New policy for electric      In June 2025, the Italian government announced the launch
                                             vehicle “trade-ins”           of a new green incentive program to encourage the
                                                                           replacement of gasoline-powered vehicles with electric
                                                                           vehicles. The program has been approved by the European
                                                                           Union and included in the fifth revision of the National
                                                                           Recovery and Resilience Plan (PNRR). The goal of this
                                                                           new policy is to replace approximately 39,000 gasoline-
                                                                           powered vehicles with electric vehicles. The government
                                                                           has allocated a budget of €600 million originally intended for
                                                                           the installation of charging stations. The funds must be fully
                                                                           utilized by 30 June 2026.
                                                                        Subsidy criteria: ISEE (annual household income)
                                                                        Subsidy amount: Taxpayers with an ISEE of €30,000 or less
                                                                           are eligible for a subsidy of €11,000 for an electric vehicle;
                                                                           taxpayers with an ISEE between €30,000 and €40,000
                                                                           receive a subsidy of €9,000.

                                                                        制：僅自發自用的小型光伏系統，且裝機功率≤7.5kW，
                                                                        可以申請使用快速通道程序；2）grid zero項目豁免：免除

           Brazil                          Normative Resolution No.   Three exemptions are provided for grid connection reviews
                                             1000/2021                  of distributed power generation: 1) fast-track mechanism:
                                                                        small-scale photovoltaic systems that generate and
                                                                        consume their own electricity, with an installed capacity
                                                                        of ≤7.5 kW, may apply to use the fast-track procedure; 2)
                                                                        grid zero project exemption: micro-generation and small-
                                                                        scale distributed power generation that does not inject
                                                                        electricity into the power distribution grid (grid zero) are
                                                                        exempt from grid connection reviews; 3) micro-distributed
                                                                        power generation systems that meet free standards may
                                                                        be exempted from grid connection review if their power
                                                                        generation capacity matches the electricity consumption of
                                                                        the user unit during power generation.
                                           14300號法律                   1）新建和現有的微型、小型光伏系統將自2023年起逐步引
                                                                        入電網費用，通過提高資產折舊額、維護成本費用等額
                                                                        外費用所佔比例實現光伏補貼退坡，從23年起逐年增加
                                                                        15%比例，直至2029年全面適用。2）低於5MW的光伏項
                                                                        目將在2045年前享有淨計量機制。
                                           Law No. 14300              1) New and existing micro and small-scale photovoltaic
                                                                        systems will gradually introduce grid fees starting in
                                                                        2023. This will be achieved by increasing the proportion
                                                                        of additional costs such as asset depreciation and
                                                                        maintenance costs, thereby phasing out photovoltaic
                                                                        subsidies. The proportion will increase by 15% annually
                                                                        starting in 2023, until it is fully applicable by 2029.
                                                                        2) Photovoltaic projects below 5MW will enjoy net
                                                                        measurement mechanisms until 2045.

                                                    capacity to provide power. If the storage duration is 5 hours
                                                    or more, the full capacity of the energy storage system
                                                    can be fed into the grid. Energy storage devices will be
                                                    permitted to charge from the grid. The new round of energy
                                                    tenders will provide additional nighttime feed-in incentives
                                                    for energy storage systems with a duration of 4 hours or
                                                    more. Independent energy storage systems can directly
                                                    generate revenue in Chile’s national electricity market
                                                    without relying on renewable energy systems.

7.   低空經濟市場分析                        7.   Low-Altitude Economy Market Analysis

     從2024年 起，低 空 經 濟 連 續 兩 年 被           Since 2024, the low-altitude economy has been included in the
     寫 入 政 府 工 作 報 告，低 空 經 濟 作 為 戰        government work report for two consecutive years. As a strategic
     略 性 新 興 產 業，正 迎 來 前 所 未 有 的 發        emerging industry, the low-altitude economy is now facing
     展 機 遇。依 託 低 空 空 域 資 源，涵 蓋 載          unprecedented development opportunities. Leveraging low-altitude
     人 飛 行、物 流 配 送、城 市 管 理 等 多 元          airspace resources, the low-altitude economy, which encompasses
     應 用 場 景 的 低 空 經 濟，在 航 空 技 術、         diverse application scenarios such as manned flight, logistics
     智 慧 空 管 等 核 心 支 撐 下 加 速 崛 起。低        delivery, and urban management, rapidly emerging with the support
     空經濟正在為區域經濟增長注入新動                     of core technologies like aviation technology and intelligent air traffic
     力，也為人們的生活帶來更多可能性。                    management. The low-altitude economy is injecting new momentum
     國 務 院 成 立 專 門 的 低 空 經 濟 司，統 籌        into regional economic growth and bringing more possibilities to

     8.    固態電池市場分析                                      8.    Analysis of the solid-state battery market

           固 態 電 池 是 一 種 新 型 儲 能 技 術，其 工                       Solid-state batteries are a new type of energy storage technology,
           作 原 理 與 液 態 鋰 離 子 電 池 相 似。固 態                       with operating principles similar to liquid lithium-ion batteries. The main
           鋰離子電池的主要材料包括正極材料、                                   materials of solid-state lithium-ion batteries include cathode materials,
           負 極 材 料 和 固 態 電 解 質 等，其 核 心 在                       anode materials and solid electrolytes. The core of this technology
           於將液態電池的電解液與隔膜替換成                                    lies in replacing the electrolyte solutions and diaphragms of liquid
           固 態 電 解 質，實 現 不 用 或 者 少 用 隔 膜                       batteries with solid electrolytes, reducing or eliminating the need
           及 電 解 液，相 較 於 傳 統 鋰 離 子 電 池 具                       for diaphragms and electrolyte solutions. Compared to traditional
           備更高能量密度（單位體積╱重量下存                                   lithium-ion batteries, solid-state batteries offer higher energy density
           儲更多電能）、更快的充電速度、更長                                   (storing more electric energy per unit volume/weight), faster charging
           的使用壽命以及更高的安全性（避免液                                   speeds, longer service life, and increased safety (mitigating risks of
           態電解液洩漏、燃燒風險），但目前這                                   liquid electrolyte leakage and combustion). However, this technology
           項 技 術 仍 面 臨 電 解 質 材 料 成 本 高、界                       currently faces challenges such as high costs of electrolyte materials,
           面 阻 抗 大、規 模 化 生 產 工 藝 不 成 熟 等                       high interface impedance, and immature large-scale production
           挑 戰，被 視 為 下 一 代 電 池 技 術 的 重 要                       processes. Solid-state batteries are considered a crucial direction for
           發展方向。                                               the development of the next generation of battery technology.

     15.   匯率波動風險及任何有關對沖活動                               15.   Exposures to risks of exchange rate fluctuation and
                                                               corresponding hedging measures

           我 們 的 業 務 位 於 中 國 內 地，且 所 有 交                       The Group business is located in Mainland China and all transactions
           易 以 人 民 幣 進 行。除 若 干 銀 行 結 餘 以                       are denominated in RMB. Most of our assets and liabilities are
           美 元 及 其 他 外 幣 計 值 外，大 部 分 資 產                       denominated in RMB, except for certain bank balances which were
           及 負 債 以 人 民 幣 計 值。以 美 元 計 值 的                       denominated in U. S. dollars and other foreign currencies. Our assets
           資產及負債主要由若干於中國內地以                                    and liabilities denominated in U. S. dollars were mainly held by certain
           外註冊成立並以美元為其功能貨幣的                                    subsidiaries which were incorporated outside Mainland China and
           附 屬 公 司 持 有，且 我 們 於 報 告 期 內 並                       adopted U. S. dollars as their functional currency, and the Group did
           無 於 中 國 內 地 進 行 重 大 外 匯 交 易。因                       not conduct any material foreign exchange transactions in Mainland
           此，我 們 於 報 告 期 內 並 無 重 大 外 匯 風                       China during the Reporting Period. In view of the foregoing, the Group
           險。                                                  had no material foreign exchange risks during the Reporting Period.

           為 應 對 操 作 風 險，本 集 團 已 經 制 訂 了                       To deal with the operation risks, the Company has prepared the
           《外 匯 套 期 保 值 管 理 制 度》，規 定 金 融                       Foreign Exchange Hedging Management System (《外匯套期保值
           衍生品交易不得單純以盈利為目的，                                    管理制度》), prescribing that transactions on financial derivatives
           且 只 能 以 集 團 自 有 資 金 開 展。董 事 會                       shall not be conducted purely for profit and shall be carried out with
           已對外匯套期保值業務規模金額設定                                    the Group’s self-owned funds only. The Board has set an annual
           年 度 上 限，現 階 段 已 訂 立 部 分 具 體 交                       cap for the scale of such foreign exchange hedging business and
           易 內 容 涉 及 普 通 遠 期 業 務。本 集 團 會                       some concrete transactions are made for the moment involving
           密 切 監 察 我 們 的 外 匯 風 險，並 會 在 有                       ordinary forward business. The Group will closely monitor our foreign
           需要時通過適當金融工具做對沖用途，                                   exchange risks and will utilize appropriate financial instruments for
           以助降低外匯風險。                                           hedging purposes when necessary to help reduce foreign exchange

     墨西哥Sonora項目的情況更新                                    Updates on Sonora Project in Mexico

     2024年5月，本 公 司 旗 下 控 股 子 公 司 贛 鋒 國                   In May 2024, Ganfeng International Trading (Shanghai) Co., Ltd., Bacanora
     際 貿 易（上 海）有 限 公 司、Bacanora Lithium                  Lithium Limited and Sonora Lithium Ltd. (“Claimants”), the controlled
     Limited、Sonora Lithium Ltd.
                               （「申請人」）就墨                 subsidiaries of the Company, initiated an arbitration proceeding against
     西哥違反投資條約損害彼等權利的若干舉措，                                Mexico before the International Centre for Settlement of Investment Disputes
     向國際投資爭端解決中心（「ICSID」）提請仲                             (“ICSID”) to challenge various measures that violated their rights under
     裁程序。爭議舉措包括墨西哥頒佈的一系列                                 investment treaties. These measures included a series of laws, regulations,
     法律法規及相關舉措，這些法律法規及相關                                 and related measures issued by Mexico, which effectively nationalized
     舉措實際上將鋰資源國有化，影響了項目的                                 lithium resources, impacted the operation of the Sonora Lithium Project
     運營，並最終以虛假理由取消了本公司的墨                                 (“Project”) and culminated with the cancellation of the mineral concessions
     西哥子公司持有的礦產特許權。                                      held by the Company’s Mexican subsidiaries on pretextual grounds.

     不限於競價交易、大宗交易）等相關法律許                                 A Shares purchased through the secondary market (including but not limited

     股計劃項下不會有新股發行。                                       by the relevant laws (the “Target Shares”). No new Shares would be issued

     劃的存續期屆滿前10日內，經出席員工持股                                terminated if not extended upon expiry. Within ten days before the expiry

             在 深 圳 證 券 交 易 所 正 式 上 市（「 A股 上                                            Shenzhen Stock Exchange (the “A-Share Listing”), and on 11 October

             市」）。本 公 司 註 冊 辦 事 處 位 於 江 西 省                                             Company is located at Longteng Road, Xinyu Economic Development
             新余市經濟開發區龍騰路。                                                              Zone, Jiangxi Province.

             於 本 期 間，本 集 團 主 要 業 務 為：深 加                                               During the period, the Group was involved in the development,

             能 電 池 等 的 研 發、生 產 和 銷 售、鋰 資                                               new materials, lithium power and energy storage batteries, lithium
             源、鋰電池回收等。                                                                 resources, lithium battery recycling, etc.

     第34號 中 期 財 務 報 告 編 製。中 期 簡 明                          with IAS 34 Interim Financial Reporting. The interim condensed
     綜合財務資料並不包括年度財務報表                                      consolidated financial information does not include all the information
     規 定 的 所 有 資 料 及 披 露，並 應 連 同 本                         and disclosures required in the annual financial statements, and
     集 團 截 至2024年12月31日 止 年 度 的 年                          should be read in conjunction with the Group’s annual consolidated
     度綜合財務報表一併閱讀。                                          financial statements for the year ended 31 December 2024.

     持續經營基礎                                                Going concern basis

     於2025年6月30日，本集團流動負債超                                  As at 30 June 2025, the Group’s current liabilities exceeded its
     出 流 動 資 產 人 民 幣9,519,845,000元。                        current assets by RMB9,519,845,000. The directors of the Company
     鑒 於 本 集 團 的 信 用 歷 史，本 公 司 董 事                         have considered the Group’s continuous available net cash flows from
     已考慮本集團的可用持續經營現金流                                      operations, current sources of funds, unutilised banking facilities, and
     量 淨 額、當 前 資 金 來 源、未 使 用 的 銀                           other sources of financing from banks and other financial institutions
     行 信 貸、銀 行 及 其 他 金 融 機 構 的 其 他                         given the Group’s credit history. The directors of the Company believe
     融 資 來 源。本 公 司 董 事 相 信 本 集 團 擁                         that the Group has adequate resources to continue operations for
     有充足的資源自2025年6月30日後不短                                  the foreseeable future of not less than 12 months from 30 June 2025.
     於12個月的可預見未來期間內持續經                                     Accordingly, the directors of the Company are of the opinion that it is
     營。因 此，本 公 司 董 事 認 為 以 持 續 經                           appropriate to adopt the going concern basis in preparing the interim

                                                               DISCLOSURES
             除於本期間之財務資料首次採納下列                                   The accounting policies adopted in the preparation of the interim
             經修訂國際財務報告準則國際財務報                                   condensed consolidated financial information are consistent with
             告 準 則 外，編 製 中 期 簡 明 綜 合 財 務 資                      those applied in the preparation of the Group’s annual consolidated
             料所採用的會計政策與編製本集團截                                   financial statements for the year ended 31 December 2024, except
             至2024年12月31日 止 年 度 的 年 度 綜 合                       for the adoption of the following amended IFRSs Accounting Standard

             國際會計準則                     缺乏可兌換性                  Amendments to IAS 21            Lack of Exchangeability
              第21號之修訂

             經修訂國際財務報告會計準則之性質                                   The nature and impact of the amended IFRSs Accounting Standard
             及影響闡述如下：                                           are described below:

             國際會計準則第21號之修訂闡明實體                                  Amendments to IAS 21 specify how an entity shall assess whether
             應如何評估某種貨幣是否可兌換為另                                   a currency is exchangeable into another currency and how it
             一 種 貨 幣，以 及 於 缺 乏 可 兌 換 性 的 情                      shall estimate a spot exchange rate at a measurement date when
             況 下，實 體 應 如 何 估 計 計 量 日 期 的 即                      exchangeability is lacking. The amendments require disclosures of
             期 匯 率。修 訂 要 求 披 露 使 財 務 報 表 使                      information that enable users of financial statements to understand
             用者能夠了解貨幣不可兌換的影響的                                   the impact of a currency not being exchangeable. As the currencies
             資 料。由 於 本 集 團 交 易 的 貨 幣 及 集 團                      that the Group had transacted with and the functional currencies of
             實體換算為本集團列賬貨幣的功能貨                                   group entities for translation into the Group’s presentation currency
             幣 均 可 兌 換，故 該 等 修 訂 對 中 期 簡 明                      were exchangeable, the amendments did not have any impact on the
             綜合財務資料並無任何影響。                                      interim condensed consolidated financial information.

             出 於 管 理 目 的，本 集 團 根 據 其 產 品 及                      For management purposes, the Group is organised into business

             告經營板塊：                                             operating segments as follows:

             (a)     金屬鋰及鋰化合物板塊：鋰製品                             (a)   Lithium metal and compound segment: manufacture and sale
                     製造、銷售及提供加工服務；                                    of lithium products, and rendering of processing services;

             (b)     鋰 電 池 板 塊：生 產 及 銷 售 鋰 電                    (b)   Lithium battery segment: manufacture and sale of lithium
                     池，並提供工商業儲能服務；                                    batteries, and rendering of Storage at users’ end services;

             (c)     鋰礦資源及其他板塊：鋰礦石、                             (c)   Lithium ore resource and others segment: exploration and sale
                     磷礦石及其他鋰產品的勘探及銷                                   of lithium ore，phosphate ore and other lithium products.
                     售。

             管理層對本集團各經營板塊的經營業                                   Management monitors the results of the Group’s operating segments
             績 分 開 管 理，以 此 決 定 資 源 分 配 及 業                      separately for the purpose of making decisions about resource
             績 評 估。板 塊 業 績 基 於 各 項 須 予 呈 報                      allocation and performance assessment. Segment performance
             的 板 塊 溢 利╱虧 損 進 行 評 價，該 板 塊                        is evaluated based on reportable segment profit/loss, which is a
             溢 利╱虧 損 以 經 調 整 的 除 稅 前 溢 利╱                       measure of adjusted profit/loss before tax. The adjusted profit/loss
             虧 損 進 行 計 量。該 經 調 整 的 除 稅 前 溢                      before tax is measured consistently with the Group’s profit before tax
             利╱虧 損 將 利 息 收 入 及 與 租 賃 無 關 的                      except that interest income and non-lease related finance costs are
             融 資 成 本 排 除 在 外。除 此 以 外，其 計                        excluded from such measurement.
             量與本集團之除稅前溢利的計量相一
             致。

             管 理 層 已 評 估 現 金 及 現 金 等 價 物、已                    Management has assessed that the fair values of cash and cash

             計入其他應付款項及應計費用的金融                                 and accruals approximate to their carrying amounts largely due to the
             負 債 的 公 允 價 值，大 致 與 該 等 工 具 的                    short-term maturities of these instruments.

             日較短。

             本集團以財務經理為首的財務部門負                                 The Group’s finance department headed by the finance manager is
             責釐定金融工具之公允價值計量的政                                 responsible for determining the policies and procedures for the fair
             策 及 程 序。財 務 經 理 直 接 向 首 席 財 務                    value measurement of financial instruments. The finance manager
             官及審核委員會報告。在每個報告日，                                reports directly to the chief financial officer and the audit committee.
             財務部門分析金融工具的價值變動並                                 At each reporting date, the finance department analyses the
             釐定在估值中應用的主要輸入數據。                                 movements in the values of financial instruments and determines the
             估 值 經 首 席 財 務 官 審 核 並 批 准。估 值                    major inputs applied in the valuation. The valuation is reviewed and
             過程及結果每年就中期及年度財務報                                 approved by the chief financial officer. The valuation process and
             告與審核委員會討論兩次。                                     results are discussed with the audit committee twice a year for interim
                                                              and annual financial reporting.

             金融資產及負債的公允價值乃以該工                                 The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are included
             具於自願交易雙方（而非強迫或清算出                                at the amount at which the instrument could be exchanged in a
             售）當前交易下的可交易金額入賬。以                                current transaction between willing parties, other than in a forced or
             下方法及假設被用來估計公允價值：                                 liquidation sale. The following methods and assumptions were used
                                                              to estimate the fair values:

             計息銀行及其他借款的非流動部分及                                 The fair values of the non-current portion of interest-bearing bank
             其他負債的公允價值是通過使用具類                                 and other borrowings and other liabilities have been calculated by
             似 條 款，信 貸 風 險 及 剩 餘 期 限 工 具 現                    discounting the expected future cash flows using rates currently

17. 金 融 工 具 之 公 允 價 值 及 公 允         17. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY OF
    價值等級（續）                             FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (Continued)
   上市股本投資的公允價值以市場報價                      The fair values of listed equity investments are based on quoted
   為 基 礎。指 定 按 公 允 價 值 計 入 其 他 全         market prices. The fair values of unlisted equity investments
   面收益表的非上市股本投資的公允價                      designated at fair value through other comprehensive income have
   值 乃 根 據 市 場 估 值 技 術 估 計，該 估 值         been estimated using a market-based valuation technique based on
   方法乃基於不受可觀察市場價格或利                      assumptions that are not supported by observable market prices or
   率 所 支 持 的 假 設。估 值 要 求 董 事 根 據         rates. The valuation requires the directors to determine comparable
   行 業、規 模、槓 桿 作 用 及 策 略 釐 定 可           public companies (peers) based on industry, size, leverage and
   資比較公眾公司（同業），並就每一識                     strategy, and to calculate an appropriate price multiple, such as
   別的可資比較公司計算適當的價格倍                      enterprise value to earnings before interest, taxes, depreciation and
   數，如企業價值╱息稅折舊前利潤（「企                    amortisation (“EV/EBITDA”) multiple and price to earnings (“P/
   業價值╱息稅折舊前利潤」）倍數及市盈                    E”) multiple, for each comparable company identified. The multiple
   率（「市盈率」）倍數。該等倍數乃按可                    is calculated by dividing the enterprise value of the comparable
   資比較公司的企業價值除以盈利計量                      company by an earnings measure. The trading multiple is then
   計 算。交 易 倍 數 其 後 根 據 公 司 特 定 事         discounted for considerations such as liquidity and size differences
   實及情況就可比較公司之間的流動性                      between the comparable companies based on company-specific
   及 規 模 差 異 等 考 慮 因 素 予 以 折 現。貼         facts and circumstances. The discounted multiple is applied to the
   現倍數適用於非上市股本投資的相應                      corresponding earnings measure of the unlisted equity investments
   盈 利 計 量，以 計 量 公 允 價 值。董 事 認           to measure the fair value. The directors believe that the estimated
   為，計 入 綜 合 財 務 狀 況 表 的 由 估 值 技         fair values resulting from the valuation technique, which are recorded
   術產生的估算公允價值及計入其他全                      in the consolidated statement of financial position, and the related
   面收益表的公允價值相關變動屬合理，                     changes in fair values, which are recorded in other comprehensive
   並認為該等項目為在報告期末最為適                      income, are reasonable, and that they were the most appropriate
   當的價值。                                 values at the end of the reporting period.

   本 集 團 與 多 個 交 易 對 手（主 要 為 信 貸         The Group enters into derivative financial instruments with various
   評級為AAA的金融機構）訂立衍生金融                    counterparties, principally financial institutions with AAA credit
   工具。衍生金融工具（包括遠期貨幣合                     ratings. Derivative financial instruments, including forward currency
   約、利率掉期及期權）採用類似於遠期                     contracts, interest rate swaps and options, are measured using
   定 價 及 掉 期 模 型 的 估 值 技 術，以 現 值         valuation techniques similar to forward pricing and swap models, using
   計 算 法 計 量，期 權 則 運 用 二 項 式 模 型         present value calculations and options are measured using a binomial
   進 行 計 量。該 等 模 式 納 入 多 項 市 場 可         model. The models incorporate various market observable inputs
   觀 察 輸 入 數 據，包 括 交 易 對 手 的 信 貸         including the credit quality of counterparties, foreign exchange spot
   質 素、外 匯 即 期 及 遠 期 匯 率 以 及 利 率         and forward rates and interest rate curves. The carrying amounts of
   曲 線。遠 期 貨 幣 合 約、外 幣 掉 期 及 利           forward currency contracts, foreign currency swaps and interest rate
   率掉期的賬面值與其公允價值相同。                      swaps are the same as their fair values.

   於2025年6月30日，衍生工具資產頭寸                  As at 30 June 2025, the mark-to-market value of the derivative asset
   的盯市價值已扣除衍生工具交易對手                      position is net of a credit valuation adjustment attributable to derivative
   違 約 風 險 帶 來 的 信 貸 估 值 調 整。交 易         counterparty default risk. The changes in counterparty credit risk
   對手信貸風險的變動對對沖關係中指                      had no material effect on the hedge effectiveness assessment for
   定的衍生工具及按公允價值確認的其                      derivatives designated in hedge relationship and other financial
   他金融工具的對沖有效性評估並無重                      instruments recognised at fair value.
   大影響。

   就按公允價值計入其他全面收益表的                      For the fair value of the unlisted equity investments at fair value
   非 上 市 股 本 投 資 的 公 允 價 值 而 言，管         through other comprehensive income, management has estimated
   理層已估計採用合理可行替代方式作                      the potential effect of using reasonably possible alternatives as inputs
   為估值模型輸入數據之潛在影響。                       to the valuation model.
